# ConnectSphere Test Case Register

This register documents all automated test cases across **Sprint 1**, **Sprint 2**, **Sprint 3**, and **Sprint 4**. It mirrors the team's Master Test Cases Register with standard **IS212 5-Path Classification** (*Happy Path*, *Negative Path*, *Boundary Path*, *Workflow Variation*, *Cross-Cutting*).

---

## Summary Statistics

- **Total Test Cases:** 626 rows in this file, recounted on 5 Nov 2026 (SCRUM-98; +13 SCRUM-89; +24 SCRUM-92; +5 SCRUM-91; +16 SCRUM-88; +5 SCRUM-90; +10 GitHub #29; +2 SCRUM-97; +14 SCRUM-94; +24 SCRUM-95, +16 SCRUM-96 and +18 SCRUM-93, added earlier without a recount; +2 Vercel deployment; +2 SCRUM-96, TC-9617 and TC-9618, in the PR 47 merge; +93 for PR 47's tests and its follow-up fixes) by the Build / Sprint column (includes 10 preserved historical workbook entries without current test implementations)
- **Sprint 1:** 40 Test Cases (SCRUM-16: 6, SCRUM-22 & 27: 7, SCRUM-21: 15, SCRUM-24: 11, SCRUM-24 & 27: 1; the 12 SCRUM-24 rows were registered in Sprint 4)
- **Sprint 2:** 121 Test Cases (SCRUM-51: 15, SCRUM-23: 18, SCRUM-63: 8, SCRUM-30: 10, SCRUM-33: 13, SCRUM-31: 21, SCRUM-32: 16, SCRUM-34: 20)
- **Sprint 3:** 219 Test Cases (SCRUM-35: 23, SCRUM-18: 11, SCRUM-36: 13, SCRUM-37: 9, SCRUM-38: 27, SCRUM-28: 14, SCRUM-80: 12, SCRUM-81: 36, SCRUM-64: 36, SCRUM-39: 23, SCRUM-30: 1; 14 rows shared across venue stories or unmapped)
- **Sprint 4:** 246 Test Cases (SCRUM-98: 2, SCRUM-89: 13, SCRUM-92: 24, SCRUM-91: 5, SCRUM-88: 16, SCRUM-90: 8, SCRUM-80 / GitHub #29: 10, SCRUM-97: 2, SCRUM-94: 15, SCRUM-95: 24, SCRUM-96: 18, SCRUM-93: 21, Vercel deployment: 2, PR 47 UI redesign: 85, SCRUM-64: 1)
- **CSV:** `TEST_CASES.csv` holds 512 of these rows. The 80 SCRUM-31, SCRUM-32, SCRUM-34 and SCRUM-39 rows are only in this file, each block pasted onto a single line (follow-up).
- **Current PR verification (PR 47 follow-up):** frontend 283/283 on a CI-like checkout (LF line endings, TZ=UTC) and the build passes, on 5 Nov 2026. The backend is unchanged from main, where the full suite passed (575). Historical statuses are preserved; they are not new execution claims.
- **Automation:** `pytest` and Node test runner; see per-case execution evidence.

## SCRUM-64 PR review: Points 3 and 4

The locally recorded PR base is `8c46316`; reviewed head is `d7efb94`. The diff adds 24 backend and 25 frontend cases (parameterizations counted separately). Eleven existing workbook IDs are reused and 38 unique IDs added; no tests, assertions, or application features were added. AC numbering below follows bullet order in the supplied `User Stories.docx`.

Functional acceptance, frontend support, cross-story integration, and migration infrastructure are distinguished in the AC column. Migration/schema checks are supporting infrastructure, not new functional ACs. Lead assignment/reassignment ownership is not established by the supplied five stories; those two existing regression cases are explicitly unmapped. Existing Lead booking permission is preserved but not inferred as a new AC.

The supplied workbook also contains TC-6408 through TC-6416 and TC-6425, whose referenced implementations are absent from this branch. Their original records/statuses are retained as history and are not part of this run or its 49 passes. No Points 1/2 work was performed. There is no failing-tests-first commit for the original implementation; this update records only actual current executions.

Add Venue/catalogue belongs to SCRUM-30; submission and pending visibility to SCRUM-35; staff review/decisions to SCRUM-36; recorded rejection information and subsequent requests to SCRUM-37; notification delivery/display to SCRUM-64. The current minimum-field form does not establish full-characteristic/search coverage for SCRUM-30, the review UI does not establish all venue-characteristic coverage for SCRUM-36 AC1, and these tests do not establish SCRUM-37 AC3 (resubmission retaining event requirements). These are coverage limitations, not new requirements or changes made here.
